#7780. Customer Solution Architect (AU, NZ, SG) at Supabase

About Supabase

Supabase is the Postgres development platform, built by developers for developers. We provide a complete backend solution including Database, Auth, Storage, Edge Functions, Realtime, and Vector Search. All services are deeply integrated and designed for growth.

About the Role

We’re seeking a Customer Solutions Architect (CSA) to help drive the adoption of Supabase across a diverse range of customers: from agile startups building towards their first launch to established brands with highly scaled workloads.

At Supabase, CSA’s are responsible for the entire customer journey, working closely with our most valuable users to:

  1. Uncover and understand business and technical requirements.

  2. Design and architect technical solutions built on the Supabase platform.

  3. Work with customers to lead proof-of-value implementations.

  4. Provide regular customer engagements to answer complex questions, unblock adoption, and optimize scaled performance.

Successful CSAs always put customers first, working closely with them to become trusted advisors who understand their needs, work as an extension of their team toward common success, and advocate on behalf of the customer within Supabase.

You'll be joining our team to support Supabase Team & Enterprise customers, where you will be expected to lead and innovate around the following areas of responsibility:

Discovery

  • Lead technical discovery to uncover business and technical objectives, set appropriate expectations of the Supabase platform, and define the criteria for success, ensuring customers can receive the guidance they need to achieve it.

  • Design, build, and present Supabase platform demonstrations to high-value customers. Leverage quality storytelling to present the value of the Supabase platform and of the entire Supabase Success team.

  • Work directly with Growth team members to size deals, provide anticipated usage estimates, and identify potential workloads supported by the Platform.

Proof-of-Value Onboarding

  • Collaborate directly with customers to determine the technical scope of a proof-of-value to ensure customers recognize the value of the Supabase platform while minimizing risk to adoption.

  • Set proof-of-value timelines, business outcomes, and success criteria that ensure a customer is comfortable proceeding with the platform once a proof-of-value has been completed.

  • Actively guide new customers through an onboarding journey, utilizing emails, docs, guides, and webinars to promote their rapid deployment and growth on the platform.

Customer Success

  • Understand immediate custom goals (i.e onboard and adoption) as well as long-term customer opportunities (i.e. optimization & identifying new workloads) to build meaningful account plans for high-value customers.

  • Deliver on synchronous and asynchronous engagements with Supabase customers, including application architecture assessments, database design and performance optimization, and proactive support to enable customers to scale with confidence.

  • Serve as an internal champion for your customers and for the platform. Work closely with Support, Product, and Engineering to provide platform feedback, identify areas of improvement, while communicating platform roadmaps to customers.

  • Invest in customer connections to build a trust-based relationship. Everyday CSA’s work with the production data stores of startups and enterprises alike. Building trust in the Success team and Supabase platform is paramount to a successful customer relationship.

Preferred Experience

  • 6+ years of relevant work experience in Solutions Architecture, Database Engineering, or similar.

  • Strong background with relational database management systems such as PostgreSQL or MySQL.

  • Background in web application development, with familiarity with Python, C# and popular JavaScript frameworks (React, Vue, Svelte) as well as Node.js.

  • Very strong communication skills, particularly of technical concepts. CSA’s at Supabase spend a significant amount of time translating business needs to technical outcomes, and technical concepts to organizational impact.

  • Well-organized and effective storyteller with meaningful interpersonal skills. CSA’s often have to navigate multiple stakeholders with conflicting opinions, and the ability to effectively mediate this type of conflict is crucial.

  • Experience with project management, business analysis and revenue operations tools. We use HubSpot, BigQuery etc., and familiarity with these platforms will help enable your success.

  • Previous experience leading customer-facing engagements to deliver meaningful technical and business value.

What We Offer

  • Fully Remote

    We hire globally. We believe you can do your best work from anywhere. There are no Supabase offices, but we provide a WeWork membership or co-working allowance you can use anywhere in the world.

  • ESOP

    Every team member receives ESOP (equity ownership) in the company. We want everyone to share in the upside of what we’re building together.

  • Tech Allowance

    Use this budget to set up your ideal work environment—laptop, monitor, headphones, or whatever helps you do your best work.

  • Health Benefits

    Supabase covers 100% of health insurance for employees and 80% for dependents, wherever you are. Your wellbeing and your family’s health are important to us.

  • Annual Off-Sites

    Once a year, the entire company gathers in a new city for a week of connection, collaboration, and fun. It’s a highlight of our year.

  • Flexible Work

    We operate asynchronously and trust you to manage your own time. You know what needs to be done and when.

  • Professional Development

    Every team member receives an annual education allowance to spend on learning—courses, books, conferences, or anything that supports your growth.

About the Team

Supabase was born-remote and open-source-first. We believe our globally distributed team is our secret weapon in building tools developers love.

  • ~400 team members

  • 60+ countries

  • 20+ languages spoken

  • Over $1B raised (including our $500M Series F)

  • 540,000+ community members

We move fast, build in public, and use what we ship. If it’s in your project, we probably use it in ours too. We believe deeply in the open-source ecosystem and strive to support—not replace—existing tools and communities.

#7781. Lead, People Operations at Atticus

The Job

In the next two years, we aim to help over 250,000 Americans get access to life-changing benefits. Behind that impact is a remarkable group of people — attorneys, engineers, client advocates, marketers, product managers, and more. The Atticus People team makes that work possible — we build the systems, policies and operations that enable us to do our best work.

We’re hiring a People Operations Lead to run the backbone of our People function: benefits, systems, payroll and multi-state compliance through our PEO, people policy, and our performance and merit cycles. You’ll be the day-to-day owner of how this work gets done — managing our vendors, keeping us compliant as we grow, running our cycles cleanly, and improving the employee experience at every touchpoint. You’ll also play a central role in the bigger decisions ahead of us — when and how we leave our PEO, how we replace our HRIS, how our policies and benefits should evolve — bringing the analysis and recommendations that shape them.

You’ll report to Amy Yu, our Head of People, and join a small, high-ownership team that cares deeply about our mission and about having real impact. The ideal candidate has run core People Operations programs at a tech-driven company and is ready to take on a broader scope somewhere the work really matters. We’ve helped hundreds of thousands of people get the benefits they deserve, and the people doing that work are who you’d be building for.

Who You Are

  • 5+ years in People Operations at a venture-backed tech company, with hands-on ownership of at least a few of: benefits, HRIS, payroll, compliance, performance cycles, and policies

  • You’re equally comfortable running a benefits analysis as you are having a tough leave conversation, and handle both with tact and clarity

  • You’ve administered an HRIS end-to-end and managed People vendors and partners; you’ve driven a system implementation or migration and know what makes one go well

  • You’ve automated and improved HR processes to be more efficient, accurate and seamless for both HR and business users

  • You operate with low ego but high ownership, and are comfortable both doing the work and figuring out how it can be improved

Salary and Benefits

The role is a rare opportunity to join a fast-growing Series C startup that doubles as a B-corp social enterprise. Our team is still small enough for you to have an outsized impact on how we operate and how our people experience unfolds.

We offer competitive pay — including equity — and generous benefits:

  • Medical and dental insurance with 100% of employee and family premiums covered

  • 15 vacation days & ~20 paid holidays each year (including two weeks at end-of-year)

  • Free membership to OneMedical

  • $600/year internet stipend

  • $1,000 reimbursable stipend for education and training outside of work

  • Up to $1,200/year student loan repayment assistance

  • 401(k) and optional HSA/FSA

  • Humble, thoughtful, smart, fun colleagues

We anticipate the base salary band for this role will be between $120,000 to $160,000 in addition to equity and benefits. The salary at offer will be determined by a number of factors such as candidate’s experience, knowledge, skills and abilities, as well as internal equity among our team.

Location

This job is fully remote and we’re committed to empowering everyone with flexibility. Work remotely, and travel to LA (on the company dime) as needed to be with your colleagues – usually quarterly, plus offsites. We care a lot about building a great culture and we think some interactions need to happen in person, so we put a lot of thought into retreats, offsites, and other ways to gather.

#7782. Software Engineer - Turso at Supabase

About Supabase

Supabase is the Postgres development platform, built by developers for developers. We provide a complete backend solution including Database, Auth, Storage, Edge Functions, Realtime, and Vector Search. All services are deeply integrated and designed for growth.

About the Role

We’re looking for a Software Engineer to join our Turso core database team and help bring forward the future of agentic databases. Turso is both an Open Source database that replaces SQLite—with an ongoing project to also support the Postgres interface—and a Cloud offering where users can create databases on demand. You’ll work closely with customers and other teams at Supabase to build the future of what databases look like in a world where users and agents are creating millions of databases a day.

This role is ideal for someone who thrives in async, fast-paced environments and is excited about building database technology tools that scale to billions.

What You’ll Be Responsible For

  • Driving the progress of the Turso Open Source database, in particular the Postgres compatibility layer.

  • Making sure code works, and never breaks, and putting the systems and guarantees in place for that to happen.

  • Maintaining the Turso Cloud operations, including the Cloud API and data plane operations.

  • Improving the scalability of the Turso Cloud operations to support a 100x larger number of databases

You Might Be a Good Fit If You

  • Have written databases before, and/or other high-stakes low-level systems like Hypervisors, OS Kernels, storage devices, etc.

  • Have experience working with remote teams and Open Source communities.

  • Have a passion and experience for correct systems that work, and have the experience in the tools to make that happen: Deterministic Simulation Testing, formal verification methods, fuzz and differential testing, etc.

  • Have experience deploying, operating and debugging large scale software systems in production in cloud environments such as AWS & Kubernetes

  • Have experience improving the observability, reliability and performance of complex cloud systems.

  • Are comfortable working in Rust.

  • Are comfortable with LLM-assisted development and evals.

  • Are comfortable working with Embedded databases.

  • Communicate clearly across both technical and non-technical audiences.

  • Are energized by solving real-world problems for developers

  • Are comfortable navigating ambiguity and moving quickly

What We Offer

  • Fully Remote

    We hire globally. We believe you can do your best work from anywhere. There are no Supabase offices, but we provide a WeWork membership or co-working allowance you can use anywhere in the world.

  • ESOP

    Every team member receives ESOP (equity ownership) in the company. We want everyone to share in the upside of what we’re building together.

  • Tech Allowance

    Use this budget to set up your ideal work environment—laptop, monitor, headphones, or whatever helps you do your best work.

  • Health Benefits

    Supabase covers 100% of health insurance for employees and 80% for dependents, wherever you are. Your wellbeing and your family’s health are important to us.

  • Annual Off-Sites

    Once a year, the entire company gathers in a new city for a week of connection, collaboration, and fun. It’s a highlight of our year.

  • Flexible Work

    We operate asynchronously and trust you to manage your own time. You know what needs to be done and when.

  • Professional Development

    Every team member receives an annual education allowance to spend on learning—courses, books, conferences, or anything that supports your growth.

About the Team

Supabase was born-remote and open-source-first. We believe our globally distributed team is our secret weapon in building tools developers love.

  • ~400 team members

#7783. GRC Analyst at Supabase

About the Role

We’re looking for a GRC Analyst to join our Compliance & Security team and help make risk management, audit and compliance a continuous part of how Supabase runs. You’ll work closely with engineering, security, IT, and legal to help us define our risk tolerance, keep our compliance programs (including SOC 2 and ISO/IEC 27001:2022) current, test controls on a rolling basis, and answer customer and vendor security reviews quickly and accurately.

This role is ideal for someone who thrives in async, fast-paced environments and is excited about building developer tools that scale to millions.

What You’ll Be Responsible For

  • Run risk assessments across the company and major product changes. Score likelihood and impact, propose treatments, and bring the results to leadership to inform risk tolerance decisions.

  • Maintain the risk register, track remediation to closure, and report status to compliance and security leadership.

  • Monitor controls on a rolling basis, using automated tests where they exist and manual testing where they don’t. Triage failures, prioritize by the risk register, and align each finding with an owner and a due date.

  • Support internal audit, whether performed by an external partner or in-house: propose scope from the risk register, supply evidence, and track findings to closure.

  • Run day-to-day operations for our compliance programs: evidence collection, control owner follow-up, and readiness ahead of each audit period.

  • Extend continuous control validation beyond what our compliance platform covers. Build custom tests with AI coding tools, work with engineering and IT to pull evidence from source systems instead of collecting screenshots, and check that each test proves what the control requires.

You Might Be a Good Fit If You

  • Have 3+ years of experience in GRC, internal audit, or security compliance at a SaaS or technology company

  • Are comfortable working with SOC 2 and ISO 27001 controls, a compliance platform such as Drata, Vanta, and evidence from systems like AWS, GCP, Google Workspace, and Okta

  • Communicate clearly across both technical and non-technical audiences

  • Have experience in async or globally distributed teams

  • Are energized by solving real-world problems for developers

  • Are comfortable navigating ambiguity and moving quickly

What We Offer

  • Fully Remote

    We hire globally. We believe you can do your best work from anywhere. There are no Supabase offices, but we provide a WeWork membership or co-working allowance you can use anywhere in the world.

  • ESOP

    Every team member receives ESOP (equity ownership) in the company. We want everyone to share in the upside of what we’re building together.

    Supabase covers 100% of health insurance for employees and 80% for dependents, wherever you are. Your wellbeing and your family’s health are important to us.

  • Annual Off-Sites

    Once a year, the entire company gathers in a new city for a week of connection, collaboration, and fun. It’s a highlight of our year.

  • Flexible Work

    We operate asynchronously and trust you to manage your own time. You know what needs to be done and when.

  • Professional Development

    Every team member receives an annual education allowance to spend on learning—courses, books, conferences, or anything that supports your growth.

Supabase was born-remote and open-source-first. We believe our globally distributed team is our secret weapon in building tools developers love.

#7784. Communications and Social Media Manager at DuckDuckGo

Who We Are

Hi, we're DuckDuckGo, the online protection company and remote-first team of 400+ on a mission to raise the standard of trust online. Founded in 2008 and profitable since 2014, our annual revenue is in the hundreds of millions USD and millions use our browser on Mac, Windows, iOS, and Android, our search engine, and the DuckDuckGo subscription. We also offer private, useful, and optional AI, including Duck.ai, which lets you chat privately with ChatGPT, Claude, and other AIs, all in one place.

 

Our culture of trust, inclusivity, and empowered project management underpins everything we do, where each team member takes full ownership of their projects, from scoping and execution to postmortem. If you're seeking end-to-end ownership of your work, you've come to the right place!

Your Team and Role

Working on the Marketing and Communications Functional Team, you'll help shape how millions of people hear about DuckDuckGo, lead projects, and own key ongoing work.

 

As a Social Media And Communications Manager, you'll write social media copy, manage social media mentions and communities, coordinate proactive announcements, manage incoming press requests, regularly report out and keep track of key performance metrics and work on related projects. You'll be the directly responsible individual (DRI) on your own projects from scoping through postmortem, collaborating closely with product, design, legal, support, and leadership.

 

Example projects include:

  • Announcing DuckDuckGo products and features, across press, social, and community channels.

  • Running a multi-week social campaign to raise awareness of a product area, from measuring the baseline and planning content through weekly reporting and recommendations.

About You

  • 6+ years in communications, including PR and social media, ideally in tech.

  • Excellent writing skills, with the ability to explain products clearly, without jargon.

  • Hands-on social media experience, including content development and community management on X, Facebook, Reddit, LinkedIn, Instagram, TikTok, Threads, and Bluesky.

  • Media relations experience, including pitching journalists, handling inquiries, and building press relationships.

  • Experience measuring social media and earned media reach, engagement, sentiment, and campaign impact and turning results into insightful reports.

  • Fluency in online culture, with strong judgment about when to join a conversation and when not to.

  • Ability to run multiple campaigns, content calendars, and approval cycles with cross-functional team members at once.

  • Proven ability to manage projects from kickoff through delivery.

Compensation

This role is paid on a regional pay scale, in local currency, and stock options:

 

  • United States: $129,000 USD annually

  • United Kingdom: £97,300 GBP annually

  • Canada: C$183,200 CAD annually

  • Europe/Rest of World: €113,750 EUR annually

 

In Spain, pay is expected to transition to the EUR scale on January 1, 2027, and in other Europe/Rest of World countries on February 1, 2027.

 

Eligibility for company-sponsored health benefits is limited to team members based in the United States. This program does not extend to team members located in other countries, such as Canada or the UK.

 

Our Team Member Support Guide explains how we prioritize your wellbeing including paid parental leave, office setup, and co-working allowances.

 

Hiring Process

Hiring works best when it's a two-way street. Learn how we help you get to know DuckDuckGo, envision your future role here, and find out more about how we hire.

 

Diversity, Equity and Inclusion

DuckDuckGo provides equal work opportunities to all team members and applicants, and it prohibits discrimination and harassment of any type on the basis of race, color, ethnicity, caste, religion, age, sex (including pregnancy), national origin, disability status, genetics, protected veteran status, sexual orientation, gender identity or expression, or any other characteristic protected by our policies or federal, state, or local laws.

 

We want to ensure that our hiring process is accessible. If you need reasonable accommodation for any part of the application process because of a medical condition or disability, please send an email to careers@duckduckgo.com to let us know the nature of your request.

 

Please note that:

  • You’ll be required to attend meetings on camera via video conferencing.

  • Expect to travel at least two times a year: once for our all-hands meetup and again for a team retreat (each around 4-5 days). While extenuating circumstances may impact attendance, everyone is strongly encouraged to attend.

  • While we offer a flexible work arrangement with no core hours, expect an average full-time commitment of 40 hours per week.

  • A successful candidate must pass a background check as a condition of joining the team.

  • By applying for this role, you confirm that all information submitted is accurate and complete. You further acknowledge that providing false or fraudulent information during the application process is cause for denial of an offer, revocation of any existing offer, or other adverse action, up to and including termination after the start of your commencement of work.

 

Disclosure Statement: Use of AI in Hiring Process

As part of our commitment to enhancing our recruitment process, we utilize artificial intelligence (AI) technology to assist in reviewing and summarizing job applications and test projects, including those tools integrated into our recruitment vendor platforms. We use AI to flag potentially fraudulent applications, analyze and summarize applicants’ experience, interviews, and project performance, and help streamline our selection process.

 

Key Principles:

  • Data Privacy: All information provided in your application will be handled in accordance with our Recruiting Privacy Policy. We ensure that your personal information is protected and used solely for recruitment purposes.

  • Human Oversight and Accountability: The AI technology is designed to support our hiring team by providing insights and summaries of applications and evaluations of test projects against scoring rubrics. All final evaluations and hiring decisions, however, will be made by our hiring team, who will consider the AI's input alongside other factors.

  • Transparency: We believe in transparency regarding our hiring practices. If you have any questions about how AI is used in our recruitment process, please feel free to reach out to us.

 

By submitting your application, you acknowledge and consent to the use of AI technology in our review process. If you would like to request an alternative selection process, please contact us as at careers@duckduckgo.com. Thank you for your interest in joining DuckDuckGo!

#7785. Engineering Manager - Spark at Supabase

About Supabase

Supabase is the Postgres development platform, built by developers for developers. We provide a complete backend solution including Database, Auth, Storage, Edge Functions, Realtime, and Vector Search. All services are deeply integrated and designed for growth.

About the Role

We're looking for Engineering Managers to lead engineering effort on Supabase Spark. Supabase Spark is an initiative to build the next frontier of agentic databases, supporting billions of databases, with millions of databases created every day.

Although we don’t require managers to contribute to code directly, we do expect you to participate in design discussions and to be involved in setting the bar for engineering quality and best practices.

What You’ll Be Responsible For

Team & People

  • Build a healthy, high-trust team culture in a remote-first, globally distributed environment.

  • Run 1:1s, performance management, career development, and onboarding for your direct reports.

  • Handle people admin (leave, time off, etc.) for your team.

Hiring

  • Own the hiring plan for your team and act as Hiring Manager. First round of interviewer for all roles on the team.

  • Partner with Talent Acquisition to source, interview, and close strong candidates.

  • Contribute to headcount planning, org design, and the evolution of engineering leadership at Supabase.

Delivery & Roadmap

  • Own the team's roadmap and project management (Linear), driving accountability and clear milestones.

  • Manage workflow intake and Slack triage. These are the team’s primary inboxes for cross-team requests. You decide what gets routed where, prioritized, or pushed back on.

  • Own the team's weekly updates and keep stakeholders aligned without creating overhead.

  • Organize effort against support tickets and maintain the team's SLO.

  • Surface risks, dependencies, and tradeoffs early and clearly to leadership.

Incident Commander

  • Train as an Incident Commander. Drive coordination, resolution, and post-incident follow-up.

  • Lead blameless post-incident reviews

You Might Be a Good Fit If You

  • Have 5+ years of engineering background, with enough depth to be credible with engineers building complex systems.

  • Have 3+ years managing remote engineering teams.

  • Have managed engineering teams at a cloud, SaaS, or developer tools company.

  • Experience with developer tools or databases, plus if that includes both SQLite and Postgres ecosystems.

  • Strong technical credibility in backend and database systems: APIs, auth, and Postgres access control (roles, grants, RLS) etc.

  • Has led a team shipping a developer facing API or platform product from early stage to production.

  • Have managed remote, globally distributed teams that work asynchronously across disparate time zones.

  • Are technical enough to be credible with strong engineers, but genuinely passionate about people management. This is the work you want to do, not a track you're settling for.

  • Build psychological safety and a feedback culture: you give feedback often and in the moment, ask for it back, and create space for the team to do the same.

  • Operate as a multiplier. Focused on helping individuals achieve more than they could alone, not on being the best technical voice in the room.

  • Coach rather than direct. Remove blockers, connect people to context and resources, and trust your engineers to own how the work gets done.

  • Have a real track record of performance management and can speak to specific examples of difficult conversations and how you navigated them.

  • Are comfortable with ambiguity and capable of building structure where there isn't much.

  • Communicate clearly and write well. You're default async, default written, set context proactively, and surface risks early.

  • Are collaborative, low-ego, and adaptable. You thrive working with high-autonomy, senior ICs who have strong technical opinions and don't need to be the smartest person in the room.

  • Are comfortable with aggressive scoping and eval driven development.

What We Offer

  • Fully Remote

    We hire globally. We believe you can do your best work from anywhere. There are no Supabase offices, but we provide a WeWork membership or co-working allowance you can use anywhere in the world.

  • ESOP

    Every team member receives ESOP (equity ownership) in the company. We want everyone to share in the upside of what we’re building together.

  • Tech Allowance

    Use this budget to set up your ideal work environment—laptop, monitor, headphones, or whatever helps you do your best work.

  • Health Benefits

    Supabase covers 100% of health insurance for employees and 80% for dependents, wherever you are. Your wellbeing and your family’s health are important to us.

  • Annual Off-Sites

    Once a year, the entire company gathers in a new city for a week of connection, collaboration, and fun. It’s a highlight of our year.

    We operate asynchronously and trust you to manage your own time. You know what needs to be done and when.

  • Professional Development

    Every team member receives an annual education allowance to spend on learning—courses, books, conferences, or anything that supports your growth.

#7786. Senior SecOps Engineer at Clutch

About the Role

As a Senior SecOps Engineer at Clutch, you will shape and execute our application security and cloud security posture. You will work embedded in engineering, driving secure by design practices, threat modeling, and proactive risk mitigation across our applications and cloud infrastructure. This is a hands-on role for someone who loves building secure systems and influencing security culture in a fast-paced fintech startup.

You will act as a key advisor on secure architecture. You will own the work of identifying, fixing, and preventing vulnerabilities across our services, cloud environments, tooling, and developer workflows.

About the Team

The SecOps team is a focused group responsible for application security and cloud security across the product. We help Clutch scale securely and efficiently.

  • We partner closely with product engineering to embed security throughout the SDLC.

  • We value pragmatism, ownership, and curiosity. We always ask: “How can we make this safer and faster?”

  • We are building a security first engineering culture that enables teams to move fast and responsibly, without slowing them down.

What You’ll Do

Within 1 months, you will:

  • Assess our application and cloud security posture, and map the highest risks across services, cloud accounts, and pipelines.

  • Embed with engineering teams to review security critical features and give practical guidance on secure design.

  • Integrate developer friendly security tooling (SAST, SCA, secret scanning, IaC scanning) into our CI/CD pipelines.

  • Triage and remediate early vulnerabilities, and set a baseline for secure coding and cloud hardening.

Within 3 months, you will:

  • Lead the design and rollout of a Secure SDLC with actionable security gates and clear ownership at each stage.

  • Establish cloud security controls, including IAM least privilege, secrets management, and logging for threat detection.

  • Build internal tooling to automate threat detection, cloud posture monitoring, and vulnerability management.

  • Launch an internal security playbook with incident response runbooks tailored to Clutch’s tech stack.

Within 6 months, you will:

  • Develop and maintain secure libraries and frameworks used across engineering, such as auth modules and input validators.

  • Drive continuous cloud security improvements through policy as code, automated compliance checks, and drift detection.

  • Build and mentor a security champion network inside engineering teams.

  • Partner with developers to threat model new services and cloud architecture changes.

What You’ll Bring

  • 5+ years of experience in software/application security, ideally in fast-growing startups or fintech environments.

  • Deep knowledge of web application security, secure software design, API hardening, and threat modeling.

  • Experience integrating security into CI/CD pipelines, including use of SAST, DAST, SCA, and IaC scanning tools.

  • Familiarity with common compliance frameworks (e.g., SOC 2, GLBA, PCI-DSS) and cloud-native security (AWS, GCP preferred).

  • Strong coding background (e.g., Node.js, Python, Go) and ability to read, understand, and debug code securely.

  • Excellent communication skills, with the ability to influence without authority and foster cross-functional collaboration.

Please note that this role may evolve as our business needs change, so we appreciate your flexibility and adaptability.

What’s In It For You?

  • Remote Flexibility: Enjoy the freedom of remote work from anywhere, balancing life and career seamlessly.

  • Unforgettable Off-Sites: Twice a year, bond with colleagues in exciting destinations, fostering teamwork and fresh ideas.

  • Paid Time Off and National Holidays: Enjoy 20 PTO days yearly and the National Holidays for relaxation and rejuvenation.

  • Stock Options: Joining us means having a stake in our success, so you'll receive stock options as part of your compensation package.

  • Home Office Setup: Create your ideal workspace with a dedicated budget for home office essentials.

  • Work Trip Budget: Grow personally and professionally with a budget for work-related trips and co-working.

About Us

Clutch is a revolutionary vertical SaaS company, proudly backed by Andreessen Horowitz (A16z), aimed at revolutionizing the way Credit Unions engage and change the lives of their members. As a champion of financial well-being, we address the urgent need for affordable lending solutions in an era where the average American grapples with over $155,000 in household debt.  Unlike traditional financial institutions, Clutch develops software to turn Credit Unions into FinTech lenders and leverage their balance sheets to responsibly lend to over 130M Americans. Our mission extends beyond mere financial transactions; we strive to fundamentally enhance the way credit unions interact with their members. By integrating cutting-edge technologies and user-centric designs, we help credit unions provide seamless digital experiences that are on par with leading tech companies. This approach not only preserves but revitalizes the longstanding tradition of community and member-focused service inherent to credit unions.

Please note: This position is offered on a contractor basis. Applicants must have the necessary documentation and authorization to work in the country where the job is located. Clutch cannot provide sponsorship or assist with obtaining work permits for this role.

A Note About AI at Clutch
We love AI. We use it often and encourage our team to creatively and effectively leverage AI tools in their work. If you join Clutch, we hope you'll bring the same enthusiasm for exploring how AI can amplify impact, productivity, and innovation.

That said, during the interview process, we want to hear your thoughts. Please approach interviews without the use of AI tools—our goal is to get to know how you think, solve problems, and communicate. Once you're in the seat, bring on the prompts!

#7788. Senior Software Engineer, Backend - Distributed Systems at Camunda

Camunda is the enterprise platform for agentic orchestration, enabling organizations to coordinate AI agents, people, and systems across complex, end-to-end business processes. With built-in governance, auditability, and human oversight, Camunda gives enterprises the control they need to move AI from pilots to production — safely and at scale. Trusted by over 700 organizations worldwide, including 9 of top 10 US banks, Camunda helps enterprises boost operational efficiency, accelerate time-to-value, and deliver better customer experiences.

Fully remote and global, we are in the middle of something bigger: transforming into an AI-first organisation, built on our own platform. We use Agentic AI to automate, orchestrate intelligent processes, and elevate human contribution across every team.

Named GP Bullhound’s Top 100 Next Unicorn list, 2025 Great Place to Work certified. Visionary in 2025 Gartner® Magic Quadrant™ for Business Orchestration and Automation Technologies. ranked 3rd in Flexa's 2026 Most Flexible Companies, We’re growing fast and looking for top talent to join our team. If you want meaningful work, visible impact and put something genuinely rare on your CV, keep reading.

About the Role:

As a Senior Software Engineer, Backend on Camunda’s Distributed Systems team, you’ll help build and evolve the durable, highly available, horizontally scalable streaming platform that underpins Camunda 8’s Orchestration Cluster and powers mission‑critical processes for customers around the world.

As part of the Distributed Systems team, you will work on technically challenging and rewarding projects, such as expanding on our multi-Raft implementation, our SWIM-based cluster membership algorithm, or high performance network and disk I/O pipelines.

At Camunda, we approach engineering in a holistic manner. As such, you will form strong partnerships with product management, design, QA, and sales, and work with your peers to not only deliver but directly influence the evolution of our platform to meet our customer’s needs.

Curious about the kind of challenges you'll work on at Camunda? Watch this quick 30-minute talk from our engineers to learn more about the new Camunda Exporter and how we’re solving complex problems at scale

What You Will Do:

  • Design, build, and maintain backend services for the distributed streaming platform that powers the foundation for Camunda 8’s orchestration capabilities, ensuring durability, high availability, and horizontal scalability.

  • Lead medium‑to‑large engineering initiatives (3+ months) end‑to‑end: partner with Product to translate business problems into technical requirements, write solution designs, draft and maintain project plans, and drive timely, high‑quality delivery.

  • Help mature our disaster recovery and resilience story, including implementing and evolving an online backup/restore API and contributing to improved multi‑region and multi‑tenant capabilities for our cloud offering.

  • Design and implement developer and operator tooling that makes it easier to understand, troubleshoot, and safely operate a complex distributed system in production environments.

  • Improve engineering effectiveness by collaborating with DevOps and other Core Application teams on CI, testing, and local development workflows to reduce PR time‑to‑merge and the overhead of working on multiple features in parallel.

  • Participate in L3 support and on‑call rotations, partnering closely with Support and SRE to investigate and resolve complex, performance‑sensitive production issues, and feeding what you learn back into our product and platform.

What You Will Bring to the Role:

Must Haves:

  • 7+ years of professional experience as a software engineer, with a strong focus on backend systems and/or distributed systems.

  • Proven experience leading medium‑to‑large engineering initiatives (roughly 3+ months), breaking down ambiguous problems, writing clear solution designs and project plans, coordinating with multiple teams, and driving predictable delivery.

  • Hands‑on experience building and maintaining highly concurrent, data‑intensive software, with a deep understanding of low‑level network and disk I/O (for example, throughput, latency, backpressure, batching, and failure modes).

  • Demonstrated ability to work autonomously and navigate ambiguity – you are comfortable taking on problems you haven’t solved before, breaking them down into concrete steps, and driving them to completion with minimal supervision.

  • Strong communication and collaboration skills in a remote‑first environment: you tailor explanations to your audience, translate business requirements into actionable technical work with Product, and help teams reach clear, decisive outcomes with minimal churn.

Nice to Haves:

  • Experience building workflow automation or process orchestration software.

  • Experience with distributed systems algorithms or protocols such as Raft, Paxos, SWIM, and/or CRDTs.

  • Strong familiarity with the observability pillars (logging, metrics, tracing) and how to integrate observability into the development and incident lifecycle, ideally including experience with L3 support and on‑call.

  • Deep knowledge of the Java Virtual Machine, especially its memory model and performance characteristics in highly concurrent, data‑intensive workloads.

This role is an existing vacancy

    #7795. Senior Infrastructure Engineer II at Kit

    Kit is the email marketing platform experts choose to grow their audience, deepen their relationships, and build a business around the work they love. For coaches, YouTubers, authors, podcasters, and other creatives, there isn't a better marketing hub to rely on to grow audiences, automate email marketing, and sell digital products — all within one platform.

    More importantly, there isn't a team more committed to helping creators earn a living. We're on a mission to help creators earn $1 billion using our creator marketing platform. We have always been 100% independent and 100% remote.

    We are proud to have built a product that our customers love, and we look for people who have enthusiasm and belief in our mission, vision, and values to join our team. We're also embracing AI thoughtfully — both in how we build and how we hire to ensure our team is adaptable, innovative, and ready for what's next.

    The role

    We're looking for a Senior Infrastructure Engineer II to take ownership of a large share of Kit's core infrastructure: databases, Kubernetes, version upgrades, platform tooling, and day-to-day support. You'll also build the paved paths that let engineers, non-engineers, and AI agents test and ship changes safely.

    The infrastructure supports creators who depend on Kit to reach their audiences and earn a living. When it's healthy, nobody notices. That's the job.

    This is a full-time Individual Contributor role for an experienced infrastructure engineer who likes owning production systems end to end, takes on-call seriously, leans into automation and enjoys making other people's work easier.

     

    Your support system

    You'll report to Mojtaba Hosseini, VP of Engineering at Kit. Day to day, you'll work most closely with Kris Hamoud and Ivan Mora Perez.

    You'll also partner with product engineers across Kit, and with teams outside engineering who rely on infrastructure to get their work done. Once you're ramped up, you'll join a three-person on-call rotation, which works out to roughly one week in three.

     

    What you'll do

    • First Week: Get access to our AWS accounts, Kubernetes clusters, monitoring, and CI/CD pipelines. Read through our runbooks and architecture docs. Take part in Get To Know Yous (GTKYs) with Kris, Ivan, and the engineering teams you'll support.

    • First Month: Pick up day-to-day infrastructure support requests and start working through them with Kris and Ivan as backup. Ship your first improvement, such as a version upgrade or a fix to a gap in monitoring. Shadow on-call shifts. Write down what you learn along the way, especially the parts that only lived in someone's head or those that can be automated or improved.

    • First Six Months: Own core infrastructure areas end to end: databases, Kubernetes, version upgrades, and platform tooling. Take your full share of on-call, which you'll start by the end of your first quarter. Become the go-to person for day-to-day infrastructure questions. Find one recurring source of pages and make it go away. Ship a first paved path, such as a sandbox environment where AI agents or non-engineers can safely build and test changes.

    • First Year: You're the primary owner of a large part of Kit's core infrastructure, and you lead projects in it without needing direction. Uptime stays at 99.9% or better, page volume is down, and you've found real cost savings. Your paved paths are in regular use by engineers, non-engineers, and AI agents. You've built observability good enough for AI agents to handle some on-call load. You've documented your decisions so others can build on your work.

     
     

    What will S.E.T. you up for success

     

    Skills

    • Running production infrastructure on AWS at scale, including managed relational databases, networking, and load balancing

    • Operating Kubernetes in production: upgrades, capacity planning, and troubleshooting

    • Managing infrastructure as code and maintaining CI/CD pipelines that engineers trust

    • Building monitoring and alerting that catches real problems early and doesn't page people for noise

    • Diagnosing complex, multi-service incidents calmly, then fixing the underlying cause

    • Cutting cloud costs through right-sizing and reserved capacity without hurting reliability

    • Writing clear runbooks, docs, and decision write-ups

    • Nice to have: experience with Cloudflare, ephemeral or sandbox environments, or email sending infrastructure

    Experiences

    • Around 7 or more years in infrastructure, platform, DevOps, or SRE roles

    • Owning production infrastructure for a product where downtime costs customers money

    • Carrying a pager for systems you owned, and making that on-call rotation quieter over time

    • Leading a major upgrade or migration, such as a database or Kubernetes version upgrade, with little or no customer impact

    • Building self-service tooling or paved paths that other engineers actually adopted

    • Working on a small infrastructure team where you couldn't hand problems off to a specialist

    Traits

    • You own what you run. You spot infrastructure debt and fix it without waiting to be asked, and you follow through until it's done.

    • You build quality into the work. Upgrades have rollback plans, maintenance windows are planned around customers, and "good enough" is a decision, not a default.

    • You leverage AI effectively. AI helps you increase quality and velocity where it matters most. You know when to delegate and when to dive in, while holding accountability.

    • You make work easier for everyone else. You'd rather build a tool or write a guide once than answer the same question ten times.

    • You teach what you know. You write things down so the next person, or the next AI agent, can build on your work.

    • You work in public. You share context and tradeoffs openly, including during incidents and when things go wrong.

    • You use AI thoughtfully. It's part of how you work, and you help others adopt it without creating new risks.

    • You remember who's on the other end. An outage means a creator's launch email doesn't go out, and that shapes how you prioritize.

     

    What won't set you up for success

    • Wanting a well-defined backlog handed to you each week. Here, you'll find much of the work yourself and set priorities with Kris.

    • Preferring not to be on call. On-call is a core part of this role, shared across a team of three.

    • You are skeptical of AI’s capabilities, or conversely use AI without good judgement and accountability.

    • Wanting mostly greenfield architecture. A lot of this work is running, upgrading, and improving systems that already exist.

    • Needing layers of approval before acting.

     

    Working hours and location

    We're 100% remote with team members across the globe. We work mostly on East Coast time—so if you're based on the West Coast of the United States, Europe, or Asia, you might have a few early mornings or late evenings. We do our best to keep things reasonable, but a little time zone juggling comes with the territory.

    This role is part of a three-person on-call rotation, so during your on-call week you'll need to be able to respond to pages outside your usual working hours.

    We believe PTO should always take priority over on-call. We schedule on-call around people's lives, not the other way around. On-call is a shared responsibility, but taking time off should never be a challenge

     

    Compensation + perks + benefits

    Kit has standardized salaries based on position, no matter where you live. For this role, we're hiring at our level 4 ($197,000). Level is determined based on experience and our interview process.

     

    Perks + benefits include:

    • Profit Sharing

    • Kit equity

    • 401k with a 5% match

    • We cover up to $2,100 per month toward medical premiums, with dental and vision premiums fully covered. We offer Health Insurance plans through Aetna

    • $2,000 equipment allowance for your first two years, $1,000 budget every following two years. Company-provided laptops are issued to every Kit team member and are not included in the equipment budget

    • Individual learning + development budget ($3,500/year)

    • Creator meetup budget ($50/month) to connect with creators over coffee, lunch, or other in-person meetups

    • Gender affirming benefits

    • Childcare benefit up to $3,000 annually through JOON

    • Twenty (20) days of paid time off during each year of employment

    • Paid paid vacation: An after-tax bonus of $1,000 for taking five consecutive days of vacation where you’re fully unplugged from work

    • Ten (10) paid holidays a year

    • Two weeks of paid sick time each year, including mental health + well being days

    • Twelve (12) weeks paid parental leave and flexible scheduling in your child’s first year

    • Up to six weeks of paid bereavement leave, medical leave, and disaster after six months of employment, two weeks of each paid leave in your first six months

    • Winter Break Closure: Kit closes for a week at the end of December, giving everyone a collective break to enjoy the holiday season. Essential support services remain available, with teams coordinating to ensure coverage during this period

    • Four-week, paid sabbatical after five years with the team

    • Fantastic in-person or virtual retreats with the team twice a year

     

    How to apply

    We usually receive a large number of applications, and our goal is to follow up with you within two weeks. You’ll hear from us either way. Here’s what you can expect from the rest of our hiring process:

    1. A phone screen with our recruiter (30 min)

    2. Hiring manager interview (45 min)

    3. Take-home assignment

    4. Technical interview (60 min)

    We’ll keep you informed through every step. To get started, complete this application, including a cover letter and answers to the questions on the next page. We read every single application, and your cover letter and responses help put your experience into context.

    Kit is an equal opportunity employer

    We value diversity in all of its forms. Research tells us that applicants who are female or non-binary, as well as applicants who are people of color, are less likely to apply for roles they do not feel 100% qualified for. If you think you meet more than 50% of our requirements but fewer than 100% of them, please apply.

    #7800. Staff Engineer, Imunify360 (remote work) at CloudLinux

    CloudLinux is a global remote-first company. We are driven by our principles: do the right thing, employees first, we are remote first, and we deliver high-volume, low-cost Linux infrastructure and security products that help companies to increase the efficiency of their operations. Every person on our team supports each other and does what we can to ensure we all are successful. 

    Check out our website for more information: cloudlinux.com

    We are currently hiring Staff Engineers for various projects within the Imunify360 product.

    Imunify360 is CloudLinux's multi-layer security suite for Linux web servers: antivirus and malware cleanup, WAF, IDS/IPS, Proactive Defense, patch management (KernelCare/Patchman), and IP/domain reputation. It runs on hundreds of thousands of shared, VPS and dedicated hosting servers, under cPanel, Plesk, DirectAdmin and standalone.

    The engineering org is 60+ engineers in squads covering backend, core, server agent, malware research, WAF/WebShield, and Patchman. Core services are Python; agents and performance-critical paths are Go and Rust. We run on Hetzner and our own bare metal.

    Check out our website for more information: https://imunify360.com/

    Role Mission & Scope

    Most complex technical challenges in Imunify360 go beyond individual squads. A single detection change can impact the scanner, agent, UI, reputation backend, and release process. While squad leads own delivery within their teams, the Staff Engineer ensures technical alignment across the product.

    As a Staff Engineer, you'll be a hands-on technical leader working across the entire product, focusing on the most critical and high-impact challenges as priorities evolve. You'll drive technical decisions, improve architecture, establish engineering standards, and help teams build sustainable solutions.

    This is an individual contributor role, without direct people management responsibilities. You'll remain actively involved in development, spending approximately 30–50% of your time writing production code, rather than focusing solely on architectural design.

    While deep expertise in a particular area is valued, this role requires the flexibility to work across different components, collaborate with multiple squads, and tackle challenges wherever your expertise can make the greatest impact.

    What you will own

    Architecture and technical direction

    • Own the end-to-end architecture of Imunify360 as a product: agent ↔ server ↔ cloud backends, data flows, and the contracts between them.
    • Write and drive ADRs and RFCs for cross-squad changes. Run the Wed/Fri architecture sync together with the Technical Director.
    • Keep a living tech-debt and risk register (security / compliance / uptime) with owners and dates, and get the top items funded on the roadmap.

    Hard cross-cutting problems (examples of what lands on your desk)

    • Agent resource footprint on dense shared hosts: CPU, memory and I/O budgets for scanning, file monitoring (inotify/fanotify) and WAF, measured on real kernels, not benchmarks from elsewhere.
    • Detection pipeline quality: false positive / false negative rates, signature and ML model rollout with staged gates and rollback.
    • Upgrade and release safety across a large, heterogeneous fleet (CentOS/AlmaLinux/CloudLinux/Ubuntu, multiple control panels, old kernels).
    • Backend scale and cost of reputation, ThreatIntel and telemetry services.
    • Migrating hot paths from Python to Go/Rust where the data shows it pays off, and saying no where it doesn't.

    Engineering standards

    • Set the bar for design reviews, code review, testing strategy, observability (Prometheus/Grafana/Sentry) and secure SDLC (OWASP ASVS, CIS Benchmarks as relevant).
    • Lead or co-lead SEV1/SEV2 incidents when the cause is unclear or cross-squad; make sure the blameless post-mortem ships within 24 h and its actions get closed.
    • Drive the AI-augmented SDLC in practice: show what good agentic coding looks like (prompt specificity, context provision, review quality, tool boundaries) on real changes, not slides.

    People multiplier (no direct reports)

    • Mentor senior engineers and squad leads toward staff-level scope.
    • Act as the technical bar-raiser in hiring loops for senior and lead roles.
    • Be the engineering voice in product and sales discussions: estimates, feasibility, and no dated customer commitment without a written squad-lead estimate.

    Key Milestones

    • 30 days: Shipped at least one non-trivial change to production. Can explain the full request path from agent to backend. Has a personal list of top 10 technical risks.
    • 90 days: Owns one cross-squad initiative with an approved ADR, a measured baseline, and a staged rollout plan. Published risk register reviewed in arch sync.
    • 6 months: That initiative is in production with before/after metrics. At least one engineering standard (e.g. release gating, agent perf budget, design review template) is adopted by 3+ squads.
    • 12 months: Recognized by squad leads as the default escalation point for cross-cutting technical decisions. At least one senior engineer grown into broader scope with your mentorship.

    Requirements

    Must have

    • 10+ years building and operating production software, with 3+ years in a Staff/Principal/Tech Lead scope spanning several teams.
    • Strong production experience in Python and at least one of Go or Rust .
    • Deep Linux internals : processes, filesystems, networking, kernel interfaces (inotify/fanotify, eBPF or similar), packaging (RPM/DEB), systemd.
    • Track record of designing and running distributed systems at scale: async backends, queues, databases, caching, failure modes.
    • You have owned systems that run on customer machines (agents, daemons, on-prem software) and know what upgrade safety, backward compatibility and resource budgets mean there.
    • Security mindset: threat modeling, secure coding, handling of untrusted input (files, HTTP traffic, user content).
    • Writes clear, short technical documents and drives decisions in async, remote teams across time zones.
    • Uses AI coding tools (Cursor, Claude, MCP-based tooling) daily and can judge their output critically.

    Nice to have

    • Experience in server security: AV/malware detection, WAF (ModSecurity/Coraza), IDS/IPS, rootkit detection, CVE/patch management.
    • Experience with WordPress and Node.JS web application hosting at scale
    • ML in production for classification/detection, including evaluation and safe model rollout.
    • Observability at scale: Prometheus, Grafana, Sentry, ClickHouse or similar.
    • CI/CD ownership: GitLab CI, Jenkins, build systems for multi-distro packaging.
    • Open-source contributions in security or systems tooling.

    Benefits

    What's in it for you?

    • Stay hands-on technically while owning complex, high-impact engineering work.
    • Work on challenging Linux, automation, and cybersecurity problems across mature and newer products.
    • Influence technical direction and make meaningful architectural decisions.
    • Work independently with a high level of ownership and autonomy.
    • Fully remote work with flexible working hours.
    • 24 paid vacation days, 10 national holidays, and unlimited sick leave.
    • Private medical insurance compensation.
    • Co-working and gym/sports reimbursement.
    • Education budget.
    • Opportunity to receive a reward for innovative ideas that the company can patent.

    By applying for this position, you consent to the processing of your personal data as described in our Privacy Policy (https://cloudlinux.com/candidate-privacy-notice), which provides detailed information on how we maintain and handle your data.

    #7801. Staff Security Engineer, Imunify360 (remote work) at CloudLinux

    CloudLinux is a global remote-first company. We are driven by our principles: do the right thing, employees first, we are remote first, and we deliver high-volume, low-cost Linux infrastructure and security products that help companies to increase the efficiency of their operations. Every person on our team supports each other and does what we can to ensure we all are successful. 

    Check out our website for more information: cloudlinux.com

    We are currently hiring a Staff Security Engineer within the Imunify360 product.

    Imunify360 is CloudLinux's security suite for Linux web servers. It protects hundreds of thousands of hosting servers and the websites running on them, so it sees most large-scale web attacks early — often in the first hours.

    Check out our website for more information: https://imunify360.com/

    Role Mission & Scope

    At Imunify360, protecting customers from evolving cyber threats requires close collaboration between security research, engineering, and other teams. Responding to complex attack campaigns involves more than identifying threats — it requires understanding attack techniques, developing effective detection and mitigation strategies, and ensuring customers are protected.

    As a Staff Security Engineer, you'll take end-to-end technical ownership of complex security challenges, from initial threat discovery and analysis to detection, mitigation, and post-incident review.

    This is a hands-on technical leadership role focused on practical security engineering rather than SOC monitoring, compliance, or purely theoretical research. You'll analyze malware and exploit code, develop detection and mitigation tools, anticipate emerging attack techniques, and ensure security findings translate into production-ready detections, mitigations, and fixes.

    You'll work closely with engineering, security research, support, external partners, and the broader security community to coordinate responses and drive continuous improvements in our security capabilities.

    As an individual contributor, you won't have direct reports or people management responsibilities. All security research and testing are conducted within authorized boundaries, with clearly defined scope and responsible disclosure practices.

    Your mission is to strengthen Imunify360's ability to detect, prevent, and respond to emerging threats, delivering measurable security improvements that protect customers at scale.

    What you will own

    Malware and campaign mitigation

    • Lead response to large-scale exploitation and malware campaigns: triage, root cause, and mitigation across every protection layer of the product.
    • Reverse-engineer malware and exploits targeting Linux servers and web applications.
    • Track campaigns over time, not just individual samples: cluster indicators, connect activity to known actors and toolkits, anticipate the next variant.
    • Define and run measurable outcomes per campaign: time to detect, time to protect, cleanup effectiveness, reinfection rate.

    Security response coordination

    • Own the security incident and response process, both for threats against our customers and for vulnerabilities in our own product: severity model, runbooks, communication, escalation.
    • Coordinate with external parties — software vendors, partners, CERTs/CSIRTs, abuse desks, other security vendors. Drive responsible disclosure where we find vulnerabilities.
    • Be the technical voice in security advisories and partner briefings, together with Product and Support.
    • Triage external vulnerability reports.

    Offensive security on our own product

    • Red-team our product and infrastructure. Assume a capable attacker who already has a foothold.
    • Threat-model new features before they ship; own the security review gate for high-risk changes.
    • Turn findings into fixes with the owning teams, not just reports.

    Detection engineering and tooling

    • Raise the quality bar of detections: false-positive budgets, test corpora, staged rollout with measurement gates and fast rollback.
    • Build and automate the analysis pipeline: dynamic analysis, honeypots, telemetry-driven hunting, ML-assisted triage where it measurably helps.
    • Write production code for these tools; expect 30–50% of your time to be hands-on building.
    • Turn telemetry into intelligence: emerging exploits, attacker infrastructure, trends — shared as internal briefs and selected public research.

    People multiplier (no direct reports)

    • Mentor analysts and engineers; raise the security skill level across teams.
    • Bar-raiser in security hiring loops.

    Key Milestones

    • 30 days: Analyzed real threats end-to-end. Mapped the current response flow and where it breaks. Shipped at least one detection or mitigation to production.
    • 90 days: Security response process and severity model published and agreed. Led at least one real campaign response with a written post-mortem. Baseline response metrics in place.
    • 6 months: Time from first sighting to protection measurably reduced vs. baseline. First red-team review completed, top findings fixed. Working relationships with key external partners.
    • 12 months: Recognized internally and externally as the owner of security response. Public research or disclosures published. Teams can run a standard campaign response without you.

    Requirements

    Must have

    • 10+ years in security engineering, malware research, incident response, or offensive security, with several years leading cross-team work at Staff/Principal/Lead scope.
    • Deep hands-on malware analysis and reverse engineering skills.
    • Strong web application security and practical exploitation experience.
    • Deep Linux knowledge from both the attacker's and the defender's side.
    • Proven incident response leadership on large-scale events: you have run the response, written the advisory, and closed the post-mortem.
    • Production coding in Python plus a systems language (Go, Rust or C).
    • Experience with coordinated vulnerability disclosure and working with the security community.
    • Clear, short writing for both engineers and non-technical stakeholders; works well async across time zones.
    • Daily user of AI tools for analysis and coding, with critical judgment about their output.

    Nice to have

    • Background in hosting or large web ecosystems.
    • Detection authoring at scale (signatures, WAF/IDS rules).
    • Kernel-level monitoring and rootkit detection.
    • Threat intelligence practice and actor tracking.
    • ML for malware classification.
    • Public track record: CVEs, published research, talks, CTFs, open-source security tools.
    • Relevant certifications are a plus, not a requirement.

    Benefits

    What's in it for you?

    • Stay hands-on technically while owning complex, high-impact engineering work.
    • Work on challenging Linux, automation, and cybersecurity problems across mature and newer products.
    • Influence technical direction and make meaningful architectural decisions.
    • Work independently with a high level of ownership and autonomy.
    • Fully remote work with flexible working hours.
    • 24 paid vacation days, 10 national holidays, and unlimited sick leave.
    • Private medical insurance compensation.
    • Co-working and gym/sports reimbursement.
    • Education budget.
    • Opportunity to receive a reward for innovative ideas that the company can patent.

    By applying for this position, you consent to the processing of your personal data as described in our Privacy Policy (https://cloudlinux.com/candidate-privacy-notice), which provides detailed information on how we maintain and handle your data.
